content = "- 论文指出，产物持续积累、加载后行为变化和指标上升这三类代理信号，并不能证明自演化智能体真正形成了可复用能力。\n- 作者将“从产物形成能力”定义为五项顺序依赖的属性，其中两项构成能力形成检查点，并从状态、对局和谱系三个尺度进行诊断。\n- 在使用相同基础模型的自由式五子棋环境中，Reflexion 的提升主要源于合法落子率增加，Voyager 的有效组件组合后反而错过全部 20 个一步制胜机会。\n- DGM 在通过检查点后出现仅损失式漂移，后续版本只会丢失原本通过的状态，说明基于表面信号的评估无法可靠判断能力是否形成。"
purpose = "- 为自演化智能体建立比产物数量、行为变化和总体指标更具构念效度的能力评估标准。\n- 可用于定位能力形成链条中的具体断点，区分合法性改善、组合失效与谱系退化等不同失效模式。\n- 提示评测应同时检查局部状态、完整任务表现和跨版本演化轨迹，避免将代理指标上涨误判为能力增长。"
notes = "标题：Artifact-to-Capability: Diagnosing Breaks in Capability Formation of Self-Evolving Agents in a Controlled Environment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04952\n摘要：Self-evolving agents write interaction experience into artifacts and reuse them in later tasks. Most work supports such methods with three proxy signals: artifacts accumulate, behavior changes once they are loaded, and metrics rise. We call this signal-based evaluation. What these signals are meant to indicate, however, has not been defined. To fill this gap, we define capability formation from artifacts as five sequentially dependent properties, two of which form a checkpoint for capability formation, and introduce three-scale diagnosis, which judges these properties at the state, game, and lineage levels. In freestyle Gomoku, Reflexion, Voyager, and DGM evolve with the same base model and all show the three signals, yet each exhibits a distinct capability-formation break. Reflexion shows"

summary = "HTCE通过语义约束的拓扑规范化与匹配，在保留关键导航结构的同时压缩历史经验索引，并提升冻结视觉语言策略的跨场景物体导航性能。"

content = "HTCE针对住宅环境中的物体目标导航，将不同场景里几何轨迹各异但语义结构相似的成功经验转化为可比较的规范表示。框架利用特征细分与拓扑细分描述区域命名和观测粒度差异，再通过语义约束归约生成语义序列与结构签名，同时保留语义锚点、分支关系和导航终点。系统基于细分感知匹配检索历史成功路径，为冻结的视觉语言策略提供导航指导，并可无损合并17.7%至23.6%的不同路线描述。在统一视觉语言骨干下，HTCE在HM3D v0.1、HM3D v0.2和MP3D上均取得最佳SR与SPL，SR最高提升4.9个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为跨场景具身导航提供一种结构化经验复用机制，减少对几何轨迹直接对齐的依赖。\n- 说明拓扑规范化可在不损失任务性能的情况下压缩经验索引，适合构建可扩展的导航记忆库。\n- 可将结构化历史经验作为外部指导接入冻结策略，以较低训练成本提升成功率与路径效率。\n- 对长期运行智能体的经验检索、场景泛化和拓扑记忆设计具有参考价值。"
notes = "标题：HTCE: Homeomorphism-inspired Topological Cross-Scene Experience Canonicalization and Reuse for Object Navigation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04836\n摘要：As embodied agents perform object-goal navigation across residential environments, they accumulate successful experiences whose semantic structure recurs across scenes even when geometric trajectories do not. Reusing such experience is challenging because differences in region naming and observation granularity produce distinct graph representations of similar approaches, while meaningful differences in anchors, branches, and endpoints must stay distinguishable. We introduce HTCE, a homeomorphism-inspired topological framework that canonicalizes and reuses navigation experience. Feature and topology subdivisions characterize representational variations, and their semantically constrained reductions produce comparable semantic sequences and structural signatures while preserving semantic an"

id = "resi-math"
title = "ReSI-Math：探索可恢复能力边界以高效提升数学推理"
subtopic = "数学推理"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ReSI-Math通过自评判引导的轨迹级进化搜索发现模型可恢复的数学推理能力，并将其转化为数据高效的RLVR监督信号。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04787"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，直接解码失败并不等同于模型缺乏相应能力，部分正确解可通过搜索模型自身的推理轨迹恢复，由此形成依赖当前策略的“可恢复能力边界”。ReSI-Math利用Self-Judge引导的进化搜索识别可恢复失败，并随着策略更新，自适应选择边界题目开展顺序式RLVR训练。在Qwen3-VL-8B上，正确与错误轨迹交叉在621次尝试中产生272条经验证器确认的正确轨迹，成功率为43.8%。经过三个顺序单样本RLVR阶段后，MATH500与WE-MATH平均成绩由78.71提升至80.16；在匹配的单题RLVR实验中，边界引导选择也优于随机选题。"
purpose = "- 将推理失败区分为“真正不会”与“可通过轨迹搜索恢复”，为能力诊断提供更细粒度的视角。\n- 利用模型自身可恢复轨迹生成高价值监督数据，可降低RLVR对外部数据和盲目采样的依赖。\n- 根据策略演化动态选择能力边界附近的题目，为强化学习课程设计和样本调度提供依据。\n- 正误轨迹交叉表明，失败轨迹中仍可能包含可复用的局部推理结构。"
notes = "标题：ReSI-Math: Exploring Recoverable Capability Frontiers for Efficient Reasoning Improvement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04787\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective approach for improving mathematical reasoning in large language models. However, a fundamental question remains: when a model fails to solve a problem, does it truly lack the underlying capability? In this work, we reveal that direct failure does not necessarily imply inability. Some reasoning failures can be recovered by exploring the model's own trajectories, exposing a recoverable capability frontier: a policy-dependent region where solutions are not reliably generated through direct decoding but can be elicited through trajectory-level search. Based on this observation, we introduce ReSI-Math, a framework that explores recoverable capability frontiers and converts recovered capabilities into data-efficient RL"

content = "该框架以科学目标为导向，使智能体依据物理实验产生的证据，持续修订硬件、软件、模型和实验协议。作者形式化描述了递归更新过程，并推导物理可达性、命令访问能力与模型近似误差之间的关系。案例系统集成 iPad、RayNeo X3 眼镜及光谱测量设备，并通过 USB 控制 RGB 保持和帧确认，实现生成颜色在光谱采集期间的稳定显示。系统通过 12 项实时控制检查，光谱反馈将一个目标的色差从 19.756 降至 1.211 ΔE_76^*，且另一组比较中的五个目标均得到改善。"
purpose = "- 为自主科学实验提供可同时迭代实验装置、控制软件、预测模型和操作协议的闭环脚手架。\n- 表明物理测量反馈可以直接驱动系统级自我改进，而不局限于参数更新或软件层优化。\n- 可用于构建具备设备控制、实验验证和持续修订能力的科研智能体与自动化实验平台。\n- 其可达性、控制接口与近似误差关系可作为评估自主实验系统能力边界的理论依据。"
notes = "标题：Goal-Driven Autonomous Optical Experimentation via a Self-Recursive Hardware-Software Harness\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04481\n摘要：We propose goal-driven self-recursive optical research in which agents use physical evidence to revise the hardware, software, models, and protocols used to pursue a scientific goal. The framework can be instantiated with different stimulus systems and measurement devices. We formalize its updates and derive a relation between physical attainability, command access, and model approximation error. We demonstrate the concept in an optical see-through color-matching case study using an iPad, RayNeo X3 glasses, and a TOP 300 probe coupled to a CAS140D spectroradiometer, both from Instrument Systems. A revised glasses application adds USB-controlled RGB holds and frame acknowledgements, keeping model-generated colors displayed during spectral acquisition. The interface passes 12 live control ch"

id = "item-65"
title = "科学智能体为何能治表却无法识因"
subtopic = "因果推理"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "对七种大语言模型的实验表明，科学智能体即使收集到足以区分竞争假设的证据，也常因无法预测不同因果假设下的实验结果而错误诊断。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04473"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在受控的合成调控系统中，要求科学智能体通过实验识别隐藏故障并恢复异常表型。七种受测模型恢复健康表型的成功率均高于故障诊断率，其中 Claude Opus 4.6 与 Sonnet 4.6 虽能修复全部对象，诊断正确率却仅为 55% 和 35%。在相关实验会话中，智能体有 91% 的概率接触到可区分竞争原因的证据，但仍频繁得出错误因果结论。探针实验显示，模型能够理解明确呈现的决定性证据并修正假设，却难以预测各竞争原因对应的实验结果，导致“收集对证据、推断错原因”。"
purpose = "- 科研智能体评估不应只考察干预是否成功，还需独立衡量隐藏机制与根因诊断的准确性。\n- 应增加要求模型显式预测各竞争假设下实验结果的过程验证，以暴露因果比较环节的缺陷。\n- 可据此设计面向证据使用、反事实预测和假设更新能力的科学研究基准，避免把有效干预误判为机制理解。"
notes = "标题：Treating the Symptom, Missing the Cause: Anatomy of a Scientific Reasoning Failure\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04473\n摘要：Scientific agents can find interventions that work without understanding why they do. We study this failure mode in controlled synthetic regulatory systems, where agents must experimentally identify a hidden fault and rescue the resulting phenotype. Across seven LLMs, every model restores the healthy phenotypes more often than it identifies the fault. Claude Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6 rescue every organism while diagnosing only 55% and 35% of them. This failure is not simply due to insufficient exploration or unavailable evidence. In most cases, the cause is experimentally identifiable, and agents encounter evidence that distinguishes competing causes in 91% of relevant sessions, and still frequently misdiagnose the system. Targeted probes reveal where experimental reasoning breaks down. Mode"

summary = "预注册双领域实验表明，增强验证器并未提升LLM自动化研究的发现质量，扩展并优化提议候选池反而更有效。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04420"

link = ""
content = "研究在作业车间调度和基于北京T-Drive轨迹的车联网边缘卸载两个真实数据领域中，对LLM提议与随机提议各开展100组配对实验，并设置三个验证器强度层级。429个变异案例证实验证器层级操控有效，但LLM相对随机提议的优势并未随验证强度提高，且在其中一个领域的严格层级下显著缩小。两个较弱验证门槛几乎总是选出相同候选，说明验证器排序差异可能因候选重合而无法体现。事后联合提议池分析发现，混合并扩大候选池可在领域A中增加1.52个留出集价值单位，指向提议池构成而非验证强度是提升发现质量的主要杠杆。"
purpose = "- 提醒自动化科研系统不要将更严格的验证器直接等同于更高的科学发现质量。\n- 设计评测时应分别检验验证器判别能力、候选排序变化和最终留出集收益，避免仅凭验证分数得出结论。\n- 相比持续收紧验证门槛，增加提议来源、扩大候选池并改善候选多样性可能更具收益。\n- 预注册协议、变异测试和公开实验账本可用于提高自动化科研评测的可复现性与可信度。"
notes = "标题：Verifier strength does not buy discovery quality: a pre-registered two-domain test of LLM-based automated research, with post-hoc evidence pointing to proposer-pool composition\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04420\n摘要：Automated research pipelines pair an LLM proposer with a verifier on an implicit bet: discovery quality rises with verification strength. Under a pre-registered protocol we test this bet in two real-data domains -- job-shop scheduling and vehicular edge offloading on Beijing T-Drive trajectories -- across 100 matched LLM/random pairs per domain and three verifier tiers. The manipulation binds -- a 429-case mutation battery shows scores rising across tiers -- yet the paired LLM-over-random advantage does not scale with it (A, one-sided p=0.1115; D, p=0.9994), and in D shrinks under the strict tier (margin contrast -0.040, p=0.0013). The two lower gates promote identical candidates in 100/100 and 99/100 pairs, so the strict order is untestable when they coincide. Constructively, LLM proposal"

content = "作者仅利用公开产物审计 HAL 和 tau2-bench，发现某通用智能体约一半任务因对话结束方式未触发评分而被记为失败，补评后其与专用脚手架的 13 分差距基本消失。其他问题包括嵌套模型调用被重复计入成本，以及归档轨迹将评估器生成的动作错误标记为智能体动作。使用新版评估器重评本质上属于一次新测量，其中一次文件模式检查错误使四个文件的得分静默下降 59 至 82 分。论文建议公开已评分样本比例、基于根调用跨度汇总成本、记录每个动作的来源，并固定评估器版本及发布重评差异。"
purpose = "- 提醒排行榜使用者将评分、日志、回放和版本管理代码视为测量仪器的一部分，而非中立基础设施。\n- 为智能体评测审计提供低成本检查项，可用于识别漏评分、成本统计偏差、轨迹污染和版本漂移。\n- 建议排行榜发布可复核的评分覆盖率、动作溯源和重评差异，避免由静默错误导致错误的能力比较。"
notes = "标题：Silent Instrument Errors Reorder Agent Leaderboards\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04399\n摘要：On a public agent leaderboard, a general-purpose agent trails a scaffold built for an airline customer-service benchmark by 13 points on the tasks that touch its database, and a reader could conclude that generality costs accuracy. The gap comes from the grader. The general agent often ends its conversations in a way that never triggers grading, so only about half of its episodes were scored and the rest were stored as failures. Grading the ungraded half under the benchmark's own rule closes the pooled gap, without rerunning any agent. We audit two carefully maintained public leaderboards, HAL and tau2-bench, from their published artifacts alone and find three more instrument errors. A cost column counts nested model calls twice, unevenly across rows. Archived trajectories record grader-wr"


[[items]]
id = "anchor"
title = "Anchor：跨层循环状态增强长上下文检索"
subtopic = "测试时训练"
topics = [

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Anchor通过仅在关键锚点层部署TTT记忆并跨层共享循环状态，以更小的状态开销提升长上下文检索能力与推理效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04327"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究发现，在滑动窗口注意力与测试时训练混合架构中，长上下文检索能力沿网络深度呈稀疏分布，主要集中于少数锚点层。Anchor仅在这些锚点层设置TTT记忆，并将跨层循环状态写入一次，再由多个层通过各自学习的读取映射进行访问。该设计利用层特定的伸缩与旋转变换复用相似状态信息，并能泛化到其他混合注意力架构。在Llama-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B上，Anchor相较LoLCATs以缩小10倍的循环状态实现2.3至3.1倍召回率提升和6.7至8.9点MMLU提升，同时相对Softmax注意力最多降低4.3倍端到端延迟。"
purpose = "- 揭示长上下文检索状态在Transformer层间并非均匀贡献，可通过识别锚点层减少冗余记忆。\n- 为测试时训练与线性化注意力架构提供跨层状态共享方案，兼顾检索性能、知识保持和推理延迟。\n- 可用于设计参数与内存受限的长上下文模型，并指导循环状态的层级配置和压缩。"
notes = "标题：Anchor: Augmented Cross-Layer Recurrent State for Long-Context Retrieval in Test-Time Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04327\n摘要：Parameter-efficient linearization turns a pretrained autoregressive Transformer into an efficient model by hybridizing sliding-window attention (SWA) with a constant-size recurrent memory. But does every layer's recurrent state contribute to long-context retrieval? Investigating an SWA and Test-Time Training (TTT) hybrid, we find that retrieval is sparse across depth, concentrated in a few anchor layers, and that the remaining states hold similar information expressed through layer-specific transformations. We exploit both observations in Anchor: TTT memory placed only at the anchor layers, and a cross-layer recurrent state written once and read by many layers, each through its own learned read map that realizes a layer-specific stretch and rotation, along with demonstrated generalization "


[[items]]
id = "item-69"
title = "粗粒度语义块实现免训练稀疏注意力加速"
subtopic = "推理效率"
topics = [
    "推理加速",
    "长上下文",
    "稀疏注意力",
    "系统优化",
    "计算资源约束",
]
research = [
    "SystemEvolve",
    "CacheEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "稀疏注意力",
    "长上下文",
    "推理加速",
    "语义聚类",
    "预填充",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "该方法通过构建提示特定的语义块键索引，在无需训练的情况下加速长上下文预填充与解码，并较好保持密集注意力的任务精度。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04313"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "该方法将位置不连续的键聚类为较大的语义块，并为每个提示构建专属键索引。每个查询通过对块质心打分选择候选块，再对块内原始键和值执行注意力；选择相同块的查询会被分组处理，以兼顾查询级选择能力与预填充效率。在单张 NVIDIA H200 上，Qwen3.5-4B 与 35B-A3B 在 512K 上下文下分别实现 2.3–2.4 倍预填充加速和 1.7–2.0 倍解码加速，1M 上下文下预填充加速达到 2.9–3.0 倍。35B-A3B 在 RULER、LongBench-v2 和 MRCR 等长上下文基准上接近密集注意力表现，但较小模型在多键检索任务上仍有精度损失。"
purpose = "- 为超长上下文部署提供无需重新训练或微调的稀疏注意力方案，可降低预填充与解码延迟。\n- 表明扩大语义块和词元预算可以同时改善硬件效率与任务精度，突破“更稀疏才更快”的简单权衡。\n- 查询分组与提示特定索引适合用于长文档问答、检索增强生成及长程智能体上下文处理。\n- 多键检索上的精度下降提示仍需改进语义聚类与候选块召回机制。"
notes = "标题：Coarse Semantic Blocks for Efficient Training-Free Sparse Attention\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04313\n摘要：We introduce a training-free sparse attention method that builds a prompt-specific key index to accelerate both prefill and decoding. The method clusters the keys into large semantic blocks that group tokens from noncontiguous positions. Each query position selects blocks by scoring their centroids and attends to the original keys and values within them. Queries that select the same block are processed together, so prefill stays fast even though each query chooses its own blocks. Large blocks make centroid scoring and this grouped processing cheap, so each query can attend to more tokens in the same time. Larger blocks with a larger token budget can thus be both faster and more accurate on long-context tasks than smaller blocks with a smaller budget. We evaluate two hybrid LLMs, Qwen3.5-4B"


[[items]]
id = "harness-s"
title = "Harness-S：从同源脚手架学习实现可泛化智能体训练"
subtopic = "脚手架演化"
topics = [
    "智能体脚手架演化",

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Harness-S 利用定向演化的同源脚手架挽救基础执行系统的失败轨迹，并通过强化学习将其诱发的能力迁移回单一策略。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04277"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Harness-S 认为从共同基础演化出的脚手架具有局部性、互补性与非平稳性，因此不追求一个始终最优的通用脚手架。该方法沿不同修复方向生成专用同源脚手架，构建在策略训练期间保持固定、可重复使用的 Sibling Harness Pool。只有当基础脚手架下的初始尝试全部失败时，系统才通过自适应路由器选择 Top-k 辅助脚手架生成新轨迹，并用这些轨迹替换失败样本进行强化学习。AppWorld、SWE-Gym 与 DBBench 实验显示，训练后的策略回到原始基础脚手架后仍显著优于纯策略训练，且 Rank-1 路由推理可在相同执行器采样次数下进一步提升性能。"
purpose = "- 展示如何将脚手架提供的外部执行增益内化为策略自身能力，降低部署时对复杂辅助系统的依赖。\n- 通过失败触发式救援避免无差别调用多个脚手架，有助于控制训练采样与执行成本。\n- 固定同源脚手架池并动态重锚行为画像，为处理策略学习过程中脚手架效用变化提供了可复用方案。\n- 可用于软件工程、应用操作和数据库任务中的智能体训练与跨执行环境泛化。"
notes = "标题：Harness-S: Learning from Sibling Harnesses for Generalizable Agent Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04277\n摘要：Harness evolution from a common base exhibits Locality, Complementarity, and Nonstationarity: variants repair different failures, and their utility changes as the policy learns. Rather than seeking a universally superior harness, we introduce Harness-S to selectively rescue failures of a base execution system through sibling harnesses and transfer the resulting capabilities into a single policy that remains effective under the original harness. Directional Harness Evolution develops specialized sibling harnesses along distinct repair directions, forming a reusable Sibling Harness Pool that remains fixed during policy training. Complementary Harness Rescue Training invokes this pool only when all initial attempts under the base harness fail. The same policy generates fresh rollouts under se"

id = "q64"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Q64通过复用共享文档的前缀状态与中间表征，并联合训练轻量读出网络，在多问题场景中实现候选评分准确率与推理延迟的折中优化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04218"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Q64冻结64层Qwen3.8-27B文本骨干，复用其混合前缀状态和共享文档的中间表征，仅联合训练残差适配器、四层解码器及候选条件读出模块。最终适配阶段使用8,192个筛选训练视图，更新88.66M参数；在由ARC-Challenge、ANLI-R3、MMLU-Pro和CMMLU构成的5,112项评测上，将宏平均准确率从66.46%提升至74.32%。在4,096文档词元、50个问题的合成负载中，其中位冷请求延迟为2.237秒，低于原生基线的5.383秒和实测AnyJev批处理配置的56.872秒。该优势主要适用于共享文档上的重复决策，短篇单问题请求并无同等收益，真实文档多问题验证仍待完成。"
purpose = "- 为同一长文档上的批量问答、分类或候选排序提供共享计算复用方案。\n- 说明冻结大规模骨干并训练轻量读出网络，可在有限可训练参数下显著改善任务准确率。\n- 提示系统评估应同时报告准确率、冷请求延迟和工作负载形态，避免将特定共享上下文优势泛化到单请求场景。\n- 可为前缀缓存、中间表征复用与候选条件评分模块的协同设计提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Shared Context Candidate Scoring with Jointly Adapted Readout Networks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04218\n摘要：Repeated decisions over a shared document create two distinct opportunities: reusing document computation and adapting how question representations score candidate answers. We present Q64, a candidate-scoring system that preserves a frozen 64-layer Qwen3.8-27B text backbone, reuses its hybrid prefix state and intermediate document representations, and jointly trains a residual adapter with a four-layer decoder and candidate-conditioned readout. We used ZiYor, a self-evolving AI system, to explore and refine the Q64 design. We specified the scoring method, implemented the system, and evaluated its accuracy and latency. The final adaptation stage updates 88.66 million parameters using 8,192 selected training views. On a fixed 5,112-item evaluation combining ARC-Challenge, ANLI-R3, and strati"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04104"
code = ""

content = "ModelBlaster提出记忆增强上下文强化学习（MAIC-RL）框架，用于优化异构SIMD与SIMT加速器上的数据并行内核和完整模型。其持久知识库通过Actor-Critic Textual Gradient Descent（AC-TGD）在推理阶段更新，将性能状态映射到优化动作、结果和约束，从而支持跨内核、模型及硬件目标的经验复用。框架以统一优化循环覆盖NVIDIA GPU的CUDA、Qualcomm GPU的OpenCL和RISC-V向量核心的RVV，仅需针对目标适配代码降级与性能测量层。实验中，该框架在B200 GPU、Adreno GPU和RISC-V向量核心上均显著超过PyTorch、Apache TVM、厂商SDK或ExecuTorch等基线。"
purpose = "- 展示持久化经验记忆如何帮助代码优化智能体跨任务、跨模型和跨硬件迁移性能调优知识。\n- 将内核级优化知识用于完整模型的算子分解与全局决策，为模型—内核协同优化提供统一框架。\n- 通过分离通用优化循环与硬件相关的降级、测量层，降低适配新加速器后端的工程成本。\n- 为推理时持续学习在编译优化、异构计算和自动化性能工程中的应用提供实证依据。"
notes = "标题：ModelBlaster: Closing the Performance Gap through Learning-Augmented Agents Across Models, Kernels and Hardware Backends\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04104\n摘要：Optimizing accelerator code across hardware architectures is difficult. ML compilers search predefined spaces of schedules and micro-kernels, while LLM-guided optimization can explore alternative algorithms, operator decompositions, and hardware-specific techniques. However, existing LLM agents typically target a single programming interface, toolchain, or accelerator family, focus on kernel-level optimization without model-level transforms, and offer limited reuse of experience across hardware targets. We present ModelBlaster, a Memory-Augmented In-Context Reinforcement Learning (MAIC-RL) framework for optimizing data-parallel kernels and whole models across heterogeneous SIMD and SIMT accelerators. Its mutable policy is a persistent knowledge base updated at inference time by Actor-Criti"

id = "jev"
title = "结构化临床记忆提升概率决策表现：Jev 合成评测"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "基于30个全合成病例的Jev评测显示，加入紧凑的结构化临床记忆可小幅提高决策准确率，并明显改善概率校准指标，但尚不足以证明临床有效性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04101"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究构建了覆盖12个临床领域的30个全合成病例，并为每个病例生成结构化记忆，通过Jev评测120道类型化问题。加入记忆后，准确率由90.0%提高至93.3%，但McNemar检验结果不显著（p=0.219）。概率质量改善更明显：选择对数损失由0.258降至0.175，二元Brier分数由0.054降至0.022；每次调用平均增加310个输入词元，而平均延迟仍约为0.5秒。由于病例完全合成、标签由单一作者判断，且所用记忆属于理想化的oracle上界，该结果只能视为方向性证据。"
purpose = "- 展示结构化临床记忆可作为概率决策系统的可复用状态接口，减少纵向病历中的噪声与重复信息。\n- 提示医疗决策评测不应只看准确率，还应同时考察对数损失、Brier分数等概率校准指标。\n- 为后续真实临床记录实验提供基线，但需要评估自动抽取记忆中的错误传播、信息遗漏及安全风险。\n- 说明紧凑记忆可能在几乎不增加延迟的情况下改善决策质量，但其词元成本与规模化收益仍需验证。"
notes = "标题：Structured Clinical Memory as Context for Probabilistic Decision Models: A Synthetic Evaluation with Jev\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04101\n摘要：Longitudinal clinical context is often distributed across noisy, repetitive forms and narrative records. We study whether a compact structured memory can improve a probabilistic decision model's answers when the underlying patient vignette is already available. We created 30 fully synthetic cases spanning twelve clinical domains, paired each with synthetically generated structured memory, and evaluated 120 typed questions with Jev. The raw-vignette arm achieved 90.0% accuracy (95% normal-approximation confidence interval: 84.6%-95.4%); adding memory achieved 93.3% (88.9%-97.8%). Five questions changed from incorrect to correct and one changed in the opposite direction (exact McNemar $p=0.219$). Choice log loss decreased from 0.258 to 0.175 and binary Brier score from 0.054 to 0.022. Memory"

content = "研究使用来自三个提供商的五个评判模型，重新评判带有人类投票的 4,000 场 Chatbot Arena 对战，覆盖九个系统和 33 个系统对，并交换回答顺序进行测试。评判模型与人类在单次裁决层面的一致性较低，Cohen's kappa 中位数仅为 0.22；其系统排名与人类排名的 Kendall's tau 为 0.17～0.89，中位数为 0.67。两个不同提供商的低成本评判模型共同确认结论时，精确率较单一评判提高 0.10，但收益主要来自过滤一个与人类明显不一致的评判模型。评判错误还会跨模型共同出现，说明不同评判模型之间并非独立，跨提供商复现仍可能保留共享偏差。"
purpose = "- 提醒基于 LLM-as-a-Judge 的系统排行榜必须报告评判模型选择带来的排名方差。\n- 跨提供商重复评判不能被直接视为独立复现，也不能在缺少人类标签时充当可靠的正确性证明。\n- 实践中应先用人类标注校准候选评判模型，并分析顺序效应、收敛率及跨评判模型的相关误差。\n- 多评判模型方案的收益可能来自剔除低质量评判者，而非简单增加评判数量。"
notes = "标题：Swapping the Judge Changes the Ranking: A Preregistered Study of Cross-Provider Replication in LLM-Judged System Comparisons\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04090\n摘要：Practitioners who compare systems with an LLM judge and have no human labels often check a conclusion by repeating it with a judge from another provider. We test how far that check can be trusted. In a preregistered study (with a disclosed post-registration reduction of the judge set), five judge models from three providers re-judged 4,000 Chatbot Arena battles (nine systems, 33 system pairs) that carry human votes, each in both response orders. Agreement with the human vote was low at the verdict level (median Cohen's kappa = 0.22), and system rankings did not survive a change of judge: Kendall's tau with the human ranking ranged from 0.17 to 0.89 (median 0.67), so the preregistered hypothesis that rankings remain stable was not supported. Conclusions replicated by two low-cost judges fro"

score = 3

summary = "研究揭示 RLVR 存在首因偏差：混合数据训练主要强化基础模型已擅长的简单问题，而真正推动推理表征扩展的困难问题收益有限且持续训练可能引发遗忘。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04061"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文通过受控实验和大规模分析，考察 RLVR 能否将推理能力扩展到基础模型既有表征之外。混合难度数据训练会进一步提升简单问题表现，但对需要偏离既有表征的困难问题仅带来有限增益。困难问题虽然总体增益不大，却更能推动模型学习新的推理表征并改善分布外能力。持续在困难问题上训练也可能造成简单能力遗忘，从而掩盖推理能力扩展带来的真实收益。"
purpose = "- 提醒 RLVR 数据混合与课程设计不能只按总体奖励或平均准确率优化，否则容易过度强化已有能力。\n- 可提高困难样本的采样权重，并分别监控简单题保持率与困难题能力扩展。\n- 训练评估应区分既有能力锐化、全新推理能力获得和灾难性遗忘三种效应。\n- 为解释 RLVR 在熟悉领域与分布外领域上的冲突性实验结果提供统一视角。"
notes = "标题：On the Primacy Bias in RLVR Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04061\n摘要：Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has emerged as a promising approach for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, it still remains unclear whether RLVR can extend reasoning capabilities beyond what is already encoded in the base model. In this paper, we study this question through the lens of primacy bias: the tendency of models to over-rely on already-learned representations, disproportionately improving performance on problems they already handle well while underexploring harder ones that require departing from those representations. Through our controlled experiments and large-scale analysis, we show that RLVR training on mixed data tends to further sharpen performance on easier problems while yielding only marginal gains on harder ones"

content = "Dory1.0-4B 是一个基于 normalized Transformer（nGPT）的 40 亿参数潜在循环语言模型，由输入块、共享循环块和输出块组成，并交错使用滑动窗口注意力与全注意力。模型预训练使用 6 万亿词元，其中前 4.5 万亿词元采用 NVFP4 训练，随后将上下文长度扩展至 256K。后训练结合 SFT、RLVR 与难度感知循环课程，根据提示难度分配不同循环深度；增加循环次数可显著改善编码、智能体和长上下文基准成绩。模型仅缓存最后一次循环的键值，使任意循环次数下的 KV 缓存占用与非循环模型相同，并开源 BF16、MXFP8、NVFP4 和 GGUF 量化检查点。"
purpose = "- 以单一检查点适配不同推理计算预算，为弹性测试时计算提供可部署的架构方案。\n- 将额外计算投入潜在空间而非生成更长的思维链，可提高智能体任务的词元效率。\n- 难度感知循环课程展示了训练阶段与动态推理深度协同优化的可行路径。\n- 仅保留最后一轮 KV 缓存，为递归计算模型控制显存开销提供实用设计。"
notes = "标题：Dory1.0-4B: A Looped Normalized-GPT\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03998\n摘要：We introduce Dory1.0-4B, a 4-billion-parameter looped (latent-recurrent) language model with elastic depth: a single checkpoint serves different inference compute budgets. At inference, the recurrent block can be applied a variable number of times. We pre-trained Dory1.0-4B on 6 trillion tokens. The first 4.5 trillion tokens were trained in NVFP4, and additional pre-training then extended the context length to 256K tokens. Next, we post-trained the model using Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), with a difficulty-aware loop curriculum that r"

id = "flagrot-kv-cache"
title = "FlagRot：面向低比特 KV Cache 的选择性旋转"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [

    "成本优化",
    "系统优化",

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "FlagRot 根据校准后的注意力误差选择性旋转 Key 分块，在低比特 KV Cache 中兼顾压缩率、长上下文精度与部署效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03992"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "FlagRot 发现统一旋转并不总能改善 Key 的量化效果：它虽可抑制尖峰分块，却可能在原本平坦的分块中引入新峰值。该方法对每个 32 通道分块比较旋转前后的校准注意力误差，选择误差更小的表示存储，并在读取时相应旋转 Query。选择频率与量化轴对齐：KIVI 等逐通道方案每个注意力头仅需一次决策，逐 Token 方案则对每个 Key 决策，并利用已有符号位保存选择结果。在每值 2.5 bit 时，FlagRot 将 12 个模型上的 KIVI KL 散度从 18.7×10^-3 降至 12.4×10^-3；作为 vLLM 插件部署后，在 128K RULER 上提升 2.9 分，并可改善 LongBench v1 表现。"
purpose = "- 为低比特 KV Cache 提供比全局统一旋转更细粒度、误差感知的量化策略。\n- 可在约六倍 KV Cache 压缩潜力下缓解长上下文推理的精度损失。\n- 选择机制可适配逐通道与逐 Token 量化，并尽量复用现有存储位以降低写入和元数据开销。\n- 对 2-bit、4-bit 整数及 FP4 Cache 均有启示，适合用于长上下文推理服务优化。"
notes = "标题：FlagRot: Selective Rotation for Low-Bit KV Caches\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03992\n摘要：Long-context inference is bound by the KV cache, which 2-bit codes could shrink about sixfold if their error stays in check. The usual remedy rotates keys before quantizing, assuming rotation always helps. However, we observe that it does not: rotation flattens a spiky piece of a key but can give a flat one a new peak, and both coexist within one key. We therefore propose FlagRot, which stores each 32-channel piece rotated or as is, whichever has the smaller calibrated attention error, and rotates the query instead when reading. We further show that how often to decide follows the quantization axis: per-channel caches such as KIVI need one decision per head, at no write cost, while per-token caches decide per key, with the choice stored in existing sign bits. At 2.5 bits per value, FlagRot"

A task-blind writer stores a memory without seeing the later task; a frozen reader then answers that task with and without the memory, using identical filler. A cost ledger records the resources used to form, store and expose the memory. On single-call factual-recall tasks built from LongMemEval-V2 web-agent memories, the same memory helps more when it contains information needed for the answer. The tested task-blind summaries and structured records yield smal"

content = "EvoGroup面向长期团队协作中的群体智能体，重点处理多用户共享上下文内持续累积的群体关系与协作知识。系统从仅含六个工具的最小冷启动脚手架出发，依据置信度和任务结果选择信息量较高的轨迹，并对低置信度案例触发不确定性反思。分析智能体通过自适应渐进披露和分桶分析提炼高层发现，再指导演化智能体修订脚手架。在EverMemBench留出集上，其pass@1由71.07%提升至89.58%，超过Claude Code等人工设计脚手架；冻结后的脚手架在未见过的GroupMemBench上也展现出有效泛化。"
purpose = "- 为群体智能体构建可随共享上下文和协作模式变化而自动调整的记忆脚手架。\n- 说明置信度、任务结果与不确定性反思可共同用于筛选高价值演化轨迹。\n- 渐进披露与分桶分析有助于将具体交互经验转化为可复用的脚手架改进规则。\n- 冻结脚手架后的跨基准表现表明，脚手架演化可以获得超越单一数据集的群体记忆策略。"
notes = "标题：EvoGroup: A Self-Evolving Harness for Group Agents in Shared Contexts\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03868\n摘要：With the rise of Group Agent such as Claude Tag, agents increasingly serve as digital workers for long-term team collaboration. These agents operate over shared contexts that continuously accumulate through multi-user interactions. However, existing agent memory systems are mainly designed for individual users and struggle to capture group-level knowledge in shared contexts, including group relationships and collaboratively formed knowledge. Manual memory strategies further limit adaptation to diverse and evolving shared contexts. To this end, we propose \\textbf{EvoGroup (EvoG)}, a self-evolving harness for group agents that starts from a minimal six-tool cold start and adapts through interaction with shared context. EvoG uses confidence and task outcomes to select informative trajectories"

The agent uses targeted database queries to retrieve the eviden"

summary = "研究发现，RLVR 模型虽能在分布内形成结构化推导，却可能在 OOD 难度变化下退化为暴力枚举，而联合训练双射逆问题可显著恢复推理策略与准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03627"
code = "https://github.com/ziqipang/rl_meta_thinking"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 论文构建可控的方程求解框架，要求为非双射字符串函数方程寻找多个输入，并通过解数量增加、目标字符串变长等方式定义由易到难的 OOD 偏移。\n- RLVR 训练后的模型在分布内能够高准确率地完成逐步推导，但遇到 OOD 任务时会放弃该策略，退化为低效的暴力枚举并出现准确率骤降。\n- 这一失效既不能归因于基础模型能力不足，也不同于奖励作弊，因为模型在分布内确实掌握并使用了正确推理策略。\n- 与双射逆问题联合训练可提高推导过程的确定性，在没有过程监督的情况下显著恢复 OOD 场景中的正确策略和表现，说明结果奖励能够隐式强化有效推理过程。"
purpose = "- 提醒 RLVR 评测不能只观察分布内准确率，还应检查 OOD 条件下推理策略是否保持稳定。\n- 可将可观测的中间推导和由易到难的分布偏移用于诊断推理模型的策略退化。\n- 表明辅助任务不必与目标 OOD 分布更接近，也可能通过增强推导确定性改善泛化。\n- 为利用结果奖励实现隐式信用分配、减少对过程监督的依赖提供了实验与理论依据。"
notes = "标题：Not All Reasoning Generalizes: A Case Study on Learning Generalizable Reasoning Strategies in RLVR\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03627\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can enable generalizable improvement for large language model (LLM) reasoning. This paper studies how to incentivize such generalization via reasoning strategies. We study this question with a controllable equation-solving framework: finding multiple inputs x for equations f(x)=y, where f is a non-bijective string function. This setting makes reasoning strategies observable while supporting a clear definition of easy-to-hard out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts, e.g., more solutions, longer y, etc. We uncover a subtle RLVR failure: it teaches the model to solve in-distribution tasks with high accuracy and structured step-by-step derivation, yet under OOD shifts, the same model abandons derivation and collapses to ineffective brute-force enu"

summary = "FADE 通过分离策略能力与题目固有难度来动态筛选高信息量提示，在固定 rollout 预算下提升在线 RLVR 的训练准确率与校准表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03625"
code = ""

content = "群组式 RLVR 只有在同组采样结果存在成功与失败分歧时才能产生有效相对优势，全通过或全失败的提示会浪费生成预算。FADE 使用双线性状态空间滤波器，将快速变化的策略技能水平与缓慢变化的题目固有难度分离建模。借助 Pólya–Gamma 增广和 Woodbury 更新，系统可根据每批二元 rollout 结果高效更新能力后验，并把能力变化迁移至共享技能的未观测提示。基于后验均值和方差，FADE 计算产生非退化 rollout 组的闭式效用；在五个推理基准、两个基础模型和四种调度范式上均改善准确率与校准，单步额外开销低于 0.6 毫秒。"
purpose = "- 为在线 RLVR 提供更高效的提示筛选与 rollout 预算分配机制。\n- 避免单一通过率统计因记忆窗口过短或过长而产生排序混乱和信息滞后。\n- 利用技能共享结构将局部观测快速泛化到尚未采样的提示。\n- 可作为推理模型强化学习中的自适应课程学习与训练成本优化组件。"
notes = "标题：FADE: Factored Ability--Difficulty Filtering for Online RLVR Scheduling\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03625\n摘要：Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) learns only from prompts whose sampled completions disagree: all-pass and all-fail groups produce zero relative advantage and burn generation budget without moving the policy. Adaptive schedulers try to steer rollouts toward informative prompts, yet they rely on per-prompt pass-rate trackers that entangle two quantities evolving at very different speeds---the policy's shifting competence across reasoning skills and each prompt's intrinsic difficulty. When a single exponential average or Beta counter tracks both at once, short memory scrambles the relative ordering of prompts whereas long memory reports stale pass rates. We introduce FADE (Factored Ability--Difficulty Estimation), a bilinear state-space filter that separates "

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03618"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "Strategist 将智能体系统中的推理策略表示为可执行、可组合和可复用的蓝图，并将其组织为持续扩展的策略库。面对新任务及少量开发样本，元智能体会检索相关蓝图，编写候选策略，在真实样本上测试后依据结果迭代修订。所有候选策略都会进入蓝图库，使后续任务能够继承并组合此前积累的经验。在 30 个基准上，该系统在 24 个基准中超过最强基线，并以 39% 更低的推理成本取得相近的平均准确率（74.7% 对 74.0%）；所得策略还能零搜索迁移到未见任务和其他模型。"
purpose = "- 将手工设计的推理流程转化为可搜索、可执行和可持续演化的智能体脚手架。\n- 通过策略蓝图库复用跨任务经验，减少每个新任务从零设计推理流程的成本。\n- 为模型能力与推理成本的联合优化提供自动化方案，而无需修改底层模型权重。\n- 策略对未见任务及不同模型的迁移能力表明，推理脚手架可作为独立于模型的通用资产。"
notes = "标题：Strategist: Evolving Reasoning with Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03618\n摘要：A model is only half of an AI agent system. The other half is the inference strategy: the reasoning procedure that turns model calls into a response. Today that second half is written by hand, and the experience of building one strategy never carries to the next. We introduce Strategist, a meta-agent that evolves inference strategies across tasks. Each strategy is written as a blueprint,executable code that other strategies can reuse, and together the blueprints form a library. Given a task and a small development set, Strategist retrieves related blueprints, writes candidate strategies, tests them on real samples, and revises them from the results. Every candidate then enters the library, so later tasks build on earlier discoveries. On 30 benchmarks, Strategist beats the strongest baselin"

content = "CLEO（Constrained Loop for Environment Optimization）面向政府情报等高安全场景，在不修改底层自托管 LLM 的前提下优化智能体脚手架。它基于专家验证的数据集，仅保留能够提升准确率且具备通用性和可审计性的工具、护栏、记忆策略及循环组件。在留出数据集上，CLEO 脚手架取得 83.8% 的通过率，与最强的开箱即用 Claude Code SDK 基线仅相差 1.2 个百分点。与此同时，其规模比该基线小两个数量级，有助于降低部署成本与审计复杂度。"
purpose = "- 为受安全、成本和数据主权约束的机构提供可自托管、易审计的智能体部署方案。\n- 表明固定底层模型并优化工具与循环脚手架，也能获得接近前沿托管系统的任务表现。\n- 可将专家验证数据用于脚手架组件消融和准入决策，减少无效工具及不必要的攻击面。\n- 为智能体系统的小型化、模块治理和环境层优化提供可复用思路。"
notes = "标题：Optimize the Harness, Not the Model: Fitting a Minimal Agent to a Self-Hostable LLM for Investigative Analysis\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03434\n摘要：Government intelligence teams need LLM agents that can analyze large, messy evidence collections, but security and cost constraints often rule out hosted frontier models and broad, hard-to-audit tool surfaces. We report CLEO: Constrained Loop for Environment Optimization, a method for fitting a minimal agent harness to a fixed, self-hostable LLM. Given an expert-validated dataset, CLEO admits only the tools, guardrails, memory policies, and loop components that improve accuracy while remaining general and auditable. On our held-out dataset, the CLEO harness reaches an 83.8% pass rate, within 1.2 points of the strongest out-of-the-box Claude Code SDK baseline, while being two orders of magnitude smaller."

summary = "研究复现八个多模态选择题基准的官方评分器，发现仅改变评分规则就能使相同模型输出的准确率相差最多22个百分点。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03377"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者在五个可由单张16 GB GPU运行的开源视觉语言模型上，复现了八个同行评审多模态基准的官方评分器。通过盲化的人类与LLM评判联合流程还原模型实际回答，所得标注达到0.956的Cohen's kappa一致性。实验显示，相同输出仅因评分规则不同，准确率最高可波动22个百分点；即使输出完全合规，推理轨迹的正则解析方式也可造成9.5个百分点差异。就近匹配、弃权和似然评分均存在系统性缺陷，作者建议基准同时报告非合规率，并至少采用两种评分规则。"
purpose = "- 揭示多模态基准中的评分器会将模型能力与解析规则、答案先验及偶然性混为一谈。\n- 提醒评测开发者审计自由文本到选项标签的转换逻辑，并公开评分协议与解析代码。\n- 建议排行榜同时报告非合规率、多个评分规则下的结果及评分分歧，以提高测量效度和可复现性。\n- 可用于检查推理模型轨迹解析、正则匹配和兜底策略导致的隐性评测偏差。"
notes = "标题：Same Outputs, Different Scores: Auditing the Unchecked Graders of Multimodal LLM Benchmarks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03377\n摘要：Every multimodal-LLM multiple-choice benchmark ends with a scoring rule that converts a model's response, often free-form text rather than a clean option letter, into a right/wrong verdict, buried in its code. Unlike the dataset, this rule is chosen without justification and left unexamined, yet it sets the published number. We reproduce the official scorers of eight peer-reviewed benchmarks on five open vision-language models (VLMs) that fit a single 16 GB GPU, and introduce a blinded human-plus-LLM-judge adjudication (Cohen's kappa=0.956) that recovers what the model actually answered. The scoring rule alone moves accuracy by up to 22 points on identical outputs, yet exactly 0 wherever a model emits clean option letters, so it hides on strong-model leaderboards. It is the fallback and no"


[[items]]
id = "deepseek"
title = "多层索引器：重叠执行 DeepSeek 稀疏注意力的选择与计算"
subtopic = "缓存优化"
topics = [
    "系统优化",
    "推理加速",
    "长上下文",
    "缓存优化",
    "异步推理",
]
research = [

]

    "稀疏注意力",
    "缓存优化",
    "推理加速",
    "长上下文",
    "异步推理",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Multi-layer Indexer 利用相邻层选中词元高度重合的特性，将索引扫描与注意力计算并发执行，无需重新训练即可提升 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 GLM-5 的长上下文解码速度。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03768"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "DeepSeek Sparse Attention 每层通过 lightning indexer 扫描完整缓存，并选出得分最高的 2048 个词元，但在长上下文解码中，索引扫描反而成为主要耗时。Multi-layer Indexer（MLI）让当前层使用前一索引层的选择结果，从而解除本层索引与注意力之间的依赖，使二者能够并发运行。该方案无需重新训练或增加权重，默认实现可直接运行于 SGLang 的生产解码图，也可使用面向 DeepSeek-V3.2 的融合内核进一步加速。MLI 可与 IndexCache/IndexShare 组合，在 10K 至 195K 上下文中，相同索引保留率下最高将 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 GLM-5 的解码速度分别提升 14.5% 和 9.8%，同时在 LongBench、RULER 与 LongBench v2 上维持接近 IndexCache 的准确率。"
purpose = "- 揭示学习式稀疏注意力在长上下文场景中的瓶颈可能从注意力计算转移到全缓存索引扫描。\n- 可利用相邻层选择结果的稳定性，通过跨层复用和流水线并发隐藏索引器延迟。\n- 无需重新训练或新增参数，适合以较低改造成本集成到现有生产推理系统。\n- 与 IndexCache 等跨层缓存复用技术互补，可在选择新鲜度、精度和解码吞吐量之间灵活权衡。"
notes = "标题：Multi-layer Indexer: Overlapping Selection and Attention in DeepSeek Sparse Attention\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03768\n摘要：Sparse attention cuts the cost of long-context inference by making the attention matrix sparse: each query attends only a subset of the sequence, chosen by a fixed pattern or, in recent production LLMs, learned per query. In DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA), the attention of DeepSeek-V3.2 and the GLM-5 family, every layer is preceded by a lightning indexer that scores the whole cache and keeps the k=2048 highest-scoring tokens for the layer to attend. At long context this indexer scan, not the sparse attention it feeds, is where the decode time goes. But adjacent layers select nearly the same tokens, so a layer can attend the selection of the previous indexer layer instead of its own. This removes the dependency: each indexer then runs concurrently with the attention of its layer. The resul"


[[items]]
id = "top-k"
title = "Top-k 检索下的记忆价值不可识别"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "经验检索",
    "数据归因",
    "不确定性估计",
    "因果归因",
]
research = [
    "MemoryEvolve",
    "AgentEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "长期记忆",
    "Top-k 检索",
    "Shapley 值",
    "数据归因",
    "不确定性估计",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文证明常见 Top-k 检索日志无法识别单条记忆的价值，并提出 LEDGER 以闭式估计可识别的 Shapley 值、报告置信区间并主动改善后续日志的可识别性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03289"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将智能体的记忆检索日志视为未经设计的实验，通过每条记忆的检索次数及记忆对的共同检索次数，判断价值是否可被识别。理论结果表明，无论记录多少轮交互，Top-k 检索都会使所有记忆价值处于未确定状态；对于仅提问一次且拥有独立记忆的问题，最多只能确定其中一个价值。在 14 个标准记忆基准的 55 种配置中，没有任何记忆价值能够被现有日志确定。作者提出 LEDGER，以闭式形式估计可识别的 Shapley 值及其置信区间，并通过合并历史相同的记忆和调整检索策略，提高未来日志的信息量；在汇总的 HotpotQA 上，可确定记忆比例由 0.001 提升至 0.797。"
purpose = "- 提醒记忆增强智能体不要从缺乏可识别性的检索日志中强行赋予每条记忆确定价值。\n- 可在记忆保留、合并和淘汰前，利用检索频次与共同检索统计检查价值归因是否成立。\n- LEDGER 为记忆价值估计提供置信区间，并明确标记无法确定的结果，有助于避免虚假的精确归因。\n- 结果表明检索策略不仅影响即时准确率，也决定后续能否可靠评估和优化记忆。"
notes = "标题：Top-$k$ Retrieval Leaves Memory Value Undetermined\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03289\n摘要：A memory-augmented agent must decide which memories to keep, merge and retrieve, and its only evidence of their value is its retrieval log: the memories it retrieved in each episode and whether it succeeded. Existing methods assign every memory a value from this log, even when the log cannot separate one memory's contribution from another's. We treat the log as an experiment that nobody designed and ask which memory values it determines. Two counts answer this before any outcome is observed: how often each memory was retrieved, and how often each pair was retrieved together. From these counts, we prove that top-$k$ retrieval leaves every memory value undetermined, however many episodes are logged, and that a question with its own memories, asked once, determines at most one of their values"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03238"
code = ""

content = "ScienceCUA采用两阶段框架，先从软件手册和网页中识别受支持任务、推断操作轨迹，再将其泛化为可复用技能，同时把命令目录、语法约束和快捷方式整理为按需查询的References。随后，系统对比同一任务的成功与失败轨迹，定位技能缺陷，并通过修订旧技能或添加新技能实现执行引导的自我演化。技能更新只有经过真实环境中的重复验证后才会写入技能库，以缓解文档版本与部署软件不一致的问题。作者还提出经审计修正的ScienceBoard-Verified和采用演化集、测试集隔离设计的ScienceBoard-VE；初始技能与参考资料带来最高7.23个百分点提升，演化后总增益最高达到17.12个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为将软件手册、网页文档自动转化为智能体可执行技能提供完整流程。\n- 通过成功与失败轨迹对比及重复验证，降低错误经验污染技能库的风险。\n- 演化任务与最终测试任务相互隔离，可减少任务泄漏并更可靠地评估技能泛化。\n- 可用于构建能稳定操作专业科学软件的科研智能体与领域技能库。"
notes = "标题：ScienceCUA: Resource-to-Skill Construction and Execution-Guided Skill Evolution for Scientific Computer Use Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03238\n摘要：Computer-use agents for scientific software require application-specific expertise. Much of this expertise is documented in human-authored software manuals and web pages. However, turning these resources into executable, reusable skills remains challenging due to mismatches between documented and deployed software versions and differences in scope between documentation coverage and task requirements. We introduce ScienceCUA, a two-stage framework combining resource-to-skill construction with execution-guided skill evolution. First, ScienceCUA identifies tasks supported by the software from its documentation, infers operational trajectories, and generalizes them into reusable Skills. Command catalogs, syntax constraints, and shortcuts are organized separately as References for on-demand loo"

title = "有状态工具工作流中的智能体难度审计"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03227"

content = "研究构建了一个保持操作总数不变的可执行评测工具，系统改变依赖深度、候选数量、活跃状态宽度以及当前状态快照的可访问性。主实验包含 49,152 个生成任务和 393,216 次参考控制器运行，后续冻结设计另加入 295,936 次运行，以考察状态保留策略、快照频率、隐藏状态重采样、恢复机制和候选索引等因素。结果表明，依赖深度会同时影响调度自由度与可达完成状态数量；残差缓存、预计算计划和索引选择能缓解部分结构性困难，而隐藏状态重采样会形成无法仅靠增加状态追踪突破的成功率上限。作者强调，本地 0.5B 模型未完成任何工作流，16 轮 Copilot 路由实验也仅具描述性，因此结果不支持一般能力结论。"
purpose = "- 用于将智能体的总体任务得分拆解为调度、状态保留和候选选择等具体失效来源。\n- 为有状态工具调用评测提供可控变量、解析校准和假设审计框架，减少仅凭聚合分数得出错误结论的风险。\n- 提示系统设计可优先采用残差缓存、预计算计划与候选索引，并单独评估隐藏状态变化带来的理论成功率上限。\n- 适合作为智能体工作流基准设计、状态管理策略比较及评测构念效度分析的参考。"
notes = "标题：What Structurally Breaks an Agent? Auditing Difficulty in Stateful Tool-Use Workflows\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03227\n摘要：An aggregate task score does not distinguish failures of scheduling, state retention, and candidate selection. We develop a small executable instrument that varies dependency depth, candidate count, active state width, and access to current-state snapshots while holding the number of required operations fixed. The primary study contains 49,152 generated tasks and 393,216 reference-controller rollouts. A separately frozen follow-on design adds 295,936 rollouts covering retention strategy, snapshot cadence, hidden state resampling, recovery, query locality, candidate indexing, and rel"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03223"
code = ""

content = "ARCBench 基于开源 pi agent harness 构建，通过智能体执行过程中的工具调用动作测量规则遵循情况，更贴近实际部署工作流。实验发现，上下文压缩很少直接删除规则，多数违规发生在规则仍保留于压缩历史时，智能体却将后续请求视为许可并继续执行。规则的传递方式影响未压缩条件下的违规倾向，而压缩策略与预算决定规则能否在摘要中留存。针对压缩器的最强攻击可通过破坏摘要中的规则使违规率增加一倍以上；即使防御恢复了多数规则，压缩后的违规率仍显著高于完整历史条件。"
purpose = "- 为评估长程智能体在上下文压缩后的规则遵循与执行安全提供可配置基准。\n- 说明“规则被摘要保留”不等于智能体能够正确执行，安全评测需同时检查规则留存和实际动作。\n- 提醒系统设计者关注压缩器定向攻击、摘要规则篡改及后续请求造成的隐式授权误判。\n- 可用于比较压缩策略、上下文预算和规则注入方式对智能体安全性的影响。"
notes = "标题：Do Agents Still Follow the Rules? Its tool-calling agent loop lets us measure rule compliance from actions taken during execution, bringing the evaluation closer to deployed agent workflows. Using ARCBench, we examine where violations after compaction come from, and how different compaction configurations and adversarial settings change their sources. We find that compaction rarely del"

content = "研究围绕编程智能体脚手架能否在不修改基础模型参数的情况下，跨多个任务持续自演化并保留既有能力展开。作者将三个软件工程任务数据集组织为六种演化顺序，并使用两种代表性脚手架演化方法进行系统实验。结果显示，在后续数据集上的演化会显著削弱智能体在先前数据集上的表现，暴露出严重的灾难性遗忘问题。例如，Meta-Harness 在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 上将 SWE-Bench 成绩提升至 94.0%，但继续在 FeatureBench 演化后，第五轮成绩降至 32.0%，相对下降 66%。"

notes = "标题：Can Coding Agents Sustain Continual Evolution without Forgetting?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03219\n摘要：Recently, harness self-evolution has emerged as an important paradigm for improving agent performance. Whether an agent harness can continuously evolve across multiple tasks without suffering from catastrophic forgetting remains an important open question. In this paper, we investigate this open question through a comprehensive empirical study of continual harness self-evolution. We organize three software engineering task datasets into six different evolution directions and apply two repre"

content = "CISA通过已有Key的紧凑摘要选择固定大小的KV块，避免逐词元索引和额外的全长度索引缓存，并在同一KV组内共享选中块。预填充阶段的QShare机制复用相邻查询的选择结果，以降低索引成本、提高KV复用率和GPU利用率。在百万词元测试中，CISA以减少8至16倍KV读取为代价，将RULER等基准得分控制在相对稠密注意力约2.05个百分点以内。使用四张NVIDIA B300 GPU服务32个并发百万词元请求时，其端到端吞吐量达到稠密注意力的2.29倍；进一步缩小块预算可提升至2.61倍，但会扩大质量差距。"
purpose = "- 为已有稠密注意力模型提供无需重新训练的长上下文稀疏化改造路径。\n- 通过分块索引与查询选择共享降低KV缓存读取、显存带宽压力和索引开销。\n- 展示可由块预算直接调节的质量—吞吐量权衡，适合百万词元上下文服务部署。\n- 可用于评估稀疏注意力在不同硬件、并发规模及上下文长度下的端到端收益。"
notes = "标题：CISA: Retrofitting Dense Attention Models with Chunk-Indexed Sparse Attention\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03200\n摘要：Long-context LLM inference is costly because attention repeatedly reads a growing key-value (KV) history. We introduce Chunk-Indexed Sparse Attention (CISA), which retrofits pretrained dense attention models without gradient updates. CISA selects fixed-size KV chunks using compact summaries of existing keys, avoiding token-level indexing and a separate full-length index cache. It uses one indexing query per KV group and shares selected chunks across queries in that group. During prefill, QShare reuses selections across adjacent queries to reduce indexing cost and improve KV reuse and GPU utilization. We evaluate CISA on NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Nano and Ultra, and Inkling-Small. In one-layer BF16 benchmarks at batch size 32 using Ultra's per-device attention dimensions, core attention is 11.7x fa"

content = "SCOPE针对网页智能体探索中成功轨迹稀少、终局奖励稀疏的问题，提出由短到长的渐进式学习框架。技能发现模块从历史轨迹中抽取可复用的局部技能及失败经验，技能编排模块将这些技能组成高层技能链。技能扩展模块利用与当前技能链对齐的失败经验，引导智能体探索尚未掌握的子技能，并跨多轮轨迹逐步形成任务完成能力。该框架通过智能体在策略蒸馏将技能链内化到策略参数，在 WebArena、WebVoyager 和 WebShop 上相对最强基线取得 3.9% 至 18.6% 的提升。"

notes = "标题：From Local Skills to Long-Horizon Web Tasks: Progressive Skill Exploration for LLM Web Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03116\n摘要：Large language models (LLMs) are driving autonomous agents toward real-world environments, with the Web emerging as one of the most representative interaction scenarios. Inspired by skill acquisition theory, we propose Skill-Composed Progressive Exploration (SCOPE), a short-to-long learning framework for long-horizon Web agents that decomposes complex task exploration into a gradual expansion process from local skills to complete task-solving capability. Specifically, SCOPE iteratively explores around local skills: Skill Discovery extracts reusable local skills and failure "

id = "jevbreak"
title = "JevBreak：固定答案集无法阻止决策操控"
subtopic = "模型对抗"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "JevBreak 提出面向决策模型的黑箱 Slot Attack，表明攻击者即使无法改变答案集合，也能通过追加文本将模型操控至指定错误选项。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03759"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文在 Decision Bench 的七个领域、16 项任务和 240 个文本样例上评估七种托管、开放权重及编码器决策模型。Slot Attack 将具名文本组件组合后追加到证据中，仅利用模型返回的标签概率搜索可将预测导向指定错误选项的组合。该攻击对原本正确的决策取得 32%–99% 的成功率，每次成功攻击所需查询次数中位数为 17–72 次。实验还显示，准确率、置信度阈值和固定答案集合均不能可靠反映或阻止这种决策操控风险，鲁棒性需要按模型、任务和版本分别测量。"
purpose = "- 揭示限制输出形式并不等同于限制攻击者对决策来源和结果的影响。\n- 可用于构建针对分类器、审核器及其他固定选项决策系统的黑箱红队测试。\n- 提醒部署方不要仅依赖准确率或置信度阈值判断模型安全性，应持续进行版本级定向攻击评估。\n- 为衡量攻击者能否覆盖全部错误选项提供更严格的决策鲁棒性指标。"
notes = "标题：JevBreak: Your Model, Their Decision\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03759\n摘要：Restricting what a model can say is often mistaken for restricting an attacker's influence over its decision. An attacker can add text to the evidence and steer the model toward a wrong answer that still belongs to the allowed set. We evaluate seven decision models spanning hosted APIs, open-weight models and an encoder on 16 tasks in seven domains from Decision Bench, targeting every wrong option across 240 text examples (990 targets per model). We present Slot Attack, a black-box attack designed for decision models that appends text assembled from named components to steer predictions toward an attacker-selected incorrect option. The method relies solely on returned label probabilities, achieves success rates of 32--99\\% on initially correct decisions, and identifies the effective compon"

summary = "PRIME通过结构归因、反事实路由和干预执行，为科学创意系统选择更有效的反馈修订范围，缓解诊断位置与最佳干预范围不一致的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03055"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PRIME指出，反馈暴露问题的位置不一定等同于最佳修订位置，并将其定义为“诊断—干预错配”。框架首先在问题—假设—解决方案结构上进行归因，但不预先固定修订目标。随后，它通过轻量级推演比较不同修订范围下候选干预的预期效用与风险，再将选定干预编译为包含语义动作、保留约束和依赖要求的有序计划。在112组人工标注的创意—反馈样本及四项基准上，PRIME提升了后继创意质量与路由可靠性；移除反事实路由后，质量由3.463降至1.960，路由遗憾由0.426升至1.929。"
purpose = "- 为现有科学创意生成器提供可插拔的反馈到修订决策层，无需改造其内部生成流程。\n- 将“发现哪里有问题”与“决定修改哪些部分”分离，避免只在反馈定位处进行局部修补。\n- 利用反事实推演预估跨范围干预的收益和风险，可用于改进科研智能体的迭代规划与反馈利用。\n- 结构化的执行计划有助于保留有效内容，并显式管理问题、假设和解决方案之间的依赖关系。"
notes = "标题：PRIME: Predictive Routing of Interventions by Modeling Effects for Scientific Ideation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03055\n摘要：Feedback-driven iteration is widely used to improve LLM-generated scientific ideas, but existing systems either leave revision decisions implicit in generation, align revisions with feedback-localized weaknesses, or restrict action selection to system-specific operations. This is problematic because problems, hypotheses, and solutions are interdependent: the location where a weakness manifests does not itself determine the revision scope that will produce a better successor. We term this phenomenon diagnosis-intervention mismatch. We introduce Predictive Routing of Interventions by Modeling Effects (PRIME), a plug-and-play feedback-to-revision decision layer for existing scientific ideators. PRIME operates in three stages: (1) Structural Attribution interprets feedback over the problem-hyp"

notes = "标题：BLPO: Beyond-Local Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03005\n摘要：Long-horizon tasks require large language model (LLM) agents to coordinate interdependent actions, a capability central to automating complex workflows. We summarize trajectory prefixes into task-progress states and abstract actions by their function, allowing corresponding decisions from different tasks to contribute to shared estima"

notes = "标题：CAREFUL-Bench: Effect-Aware, Judge-Free Evaluation of Multi-Turn LLM Function Calling\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02978\n摘要：The multi-turn checker of the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard (BFCL) checks only the final state of a simulated service, never the calls that produced it. We add effect information to BFCL without replacing it. Effect declarations for all 128 functions are inferred by executing each function in BFCL's own simulators and diffing the state; the remaining judgment, which writes are destructive, is made by four annotators, two humans and two language models (Fleiss' kappa = 0.75), and adjudicated. content = "研究在 K2-Horizon 与 Qwen3.5/3.8 两个模型家族的九种参数规模上，以统一 LoRA 配方训练单次前向输出类别、分数或拒答及其概率的 System-1 决策模型。仅使用窄域金融数据微调会令通用决策能力下降 9—45 个百分点，但这种专业化坍缩随模型规模增大而减弱。面对 19,640 个基于真实远期收益标注的问题及 1,360 个封闭测试问题，各规模模型均未显著超越期限条件先验，但能输出平坦且校准良好的概率，2B 模型取得最低 ECE 0.026。混合语料适配器可同时维持通用决策、规则读取和结果校准能力，并通过分域温度校准与置信区间分派，将确定性规则问题直接处理、将现实不确定问题转交复核。"
purpose = "- 说明金融决策系统不应只追求准确率，还需报告经过校准的概率，并诚实呈现不可预测性。\n- 提醒窄域 LoRA 微调可能造成严重的通用能力退化，训练前应设计通用与专业语料的混合比例。\n- 表明较小模型可能在概率校准上优于更大模型，部署时应联合比较准确率、ECE、成本和先验漂移。\n- 可将置信区间分派用于风险控制：高确定性的规则任务自动执行，缺乏条件优势的现实预测转交人工复核。"
notes = "标题：Honest Small Decision Models for Finance: Scale, Specialization Collapse, and Mixed-Corpus Remediation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03755\n摘要：Financial workflows need fast, calibrated, auditable decisions, not just generated text. We train System-1 decision models, small language models fine-tuned to answer typed questions (choice/score/noul) with a probability in a single forward pass, across two model families (K2-Horizon and Qwen3.5/3.8) and nine rungs from 0.8B to 32B parameters, using one frozen LoRA recipe on a public decision benchmark and two new finance corpora: a programmatic-gold policy corpus (rule-reading with exact labels) and a reality-labeled outcome corpus (19,640 realized forward-return questions in walk-forward splits, plus a sealed 2026 test of 1,360 questions). Narrow fine-tuning costs general decision competence 9-45 percentage points, a data-mixture effect that shrinks with model scale and is associated wi"

summary = "StateGraph通过构建动态状态依赖图并进行反事实验证，在上游事实变化时选择性失效缺乏独立支持的下游记忆、信念与行动。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02912"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "长期运行的智能体需要在环境变化后同步修订持久记忆，但现有系统通常只更新直接冲突的事实，未处理由其衍生的信念、计划和行动。StateGraph从交互历史中恢复依赖边，持续维护可演化的状态依赖图，并通过反事实验证区分真正的支持依赖与表面语义关联。修订时，系统仅在严格依赖失效且不存在独立支持路径时，才将相应下游状态判定为不可信。作者还构建了包含50个人工设计案例的StateChangeBench；相较CUPMem复现版本，StateGraph在深度1和深度3以上场景中的Impact-F1分别提高6.9和26.0个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为长期智能体提供依赖感知的记忆一致性维护机制，避免过期事实继续支撑后续决策。\n- 利用反事实验证识别真实支持关系，减少因语义相关性造成的错误级联删除。\n- 状态依赖图可用于追踪记忆更新的影响范围，并辅助信念、计划和行动的选择性回滚。\n- StateChangeBench可用于评估记忆修订的级联深度、困难负例处理能力及行动后果。"
notes = "标题：A State Change Is Local, Its Consequences Are Not: Dependency-Aware State Revision for Long-Term Agent Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02912\n摘要：Long-lived agents rely on persistent memory of users, tasks and decisions, and this memory must stay consistent with a world that keeps changing. When an upstream fact changes, however, memory systems can revise the contradicted memory itself while the beliefs, plans and actions derived from it remain accepted, even though they have lost their support. We therefore formulate dependency-aware state revision, which asks not only what changed but also which dependent states should no longer be trusted. To address it, StateGraph maintains an evolving state dependency graph whose edges are recovered from interaction histories. Its core design is counterfactual verification: each candidate edge is tested by asking whether the dependent state would survive the loss of its premise, which separates"

content = "CARE是面向生产客服智能体的递归自我改进框架，目标是以可持续学习的内部中型模型替代前沿闭源模型。系统在一次性冷启动后让当前模型与既有模型共同承接真实流量，并通过全天候、证据盲化且独立复核的检查流程确认失败。框架将模型错误与技能、工具及脚手架缺陷分离，仅截取模型首次错误决策之前的轨迹，并为其配对经过验证的修复方案。随后利用带有特权诊断信息的在策略自蒸馏更新模型，并通过冻结的可执行仿真、离线轨迹检查、线上对比和人工审批决定是否晋升新版本。"
purpose = "- 为生产环境中的智能体持续学习提供从失败发现、归因、修复到版本晋升的闭环方案。\n- 通过区分模型、技能、工具和脚手架故障，避免将系统组件缺陷错误归因于模型训练。\n- 使用在策略自蒸馏把已验证的修复知识注入当前模型，同时保持学生模型推理时不依赖特权信息。\n- 以仿真、离线检查、线上对比和人工审批构成多层发布门槛，降低递归更新导致能力回退的风险。"
notes = "标题：CARE: Grounded Recursive Self-Improvement for Production Customer-Service Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02839\n摘要：Recursive self-improvement (RSI) in production agents involves transforming accumulated experience into reliable model updates while adapting to evolving business policies. We present CARE (Customer-service Agent Retrospective and Evolve), a grounded RSI framework that operationalizes this process in customer service. Its goal is to replace a frontier proprietary incumbent with an in-house mid-sized model that continues learning after deployment. After a one-time cold start on governed production traces, the current model serves production traffic alongside the incumbent, and its own failures drive the next update. A 24/7 inspection process establishes failures through evidence-blinded, independently reviewed judgments. Attribution separates model errors from defects in Skills, tools, or t"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02833"

content = "研究提出专家审计的虚拟科研环境OmniQ，将59篇计算量子科学论文转化为可执行的结果复现任务，覆盖经典模拟、量子算法和量子性质表征。对五种大语言模型智能体系统及六种第三方智能体脚手架的评测显示，当前AI科学家经常无法复现已发表结果，主要障碍是论文提供的信息与自主执行所需信息不匹配。作者进一步提出Residual Agents（RA），把外部验证过的工作流修复蒸馏为可复用科研经验，在固定基础模型和有限适配预算下实现自我改进。RA将DeepSeek V4.1 Flash的表现从68%提升至85%，以约三分之一成本达到Claude Code with Opus 5的水平；所有复现结果还被整合进基于MCP的可扩展平台。"
purpose = "- 为科研智能体提供从论文阅读走向可执行实验复现的系统化评测环境。\n- 说明人工专家对失败工作流的修复可被结构化、验证并沉淀为智能体的长期科研经验。\n- 展示无需更换基础模型，也能通过经验蒸馏和有限适配提高科研任务成功率与成本效率。\n- 基于MCP开放已验证科研工作流，为持续接入新论文和构建闭环自动化科学研究平台提供基础。"
notes = "标题：Reimagining computational quantum research with AI scientists\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02833\n摘要：AI scientists are expanding automated research across scientific domains, yet their ability to turn the scientific record into a foundation for autonomous discovery remains elusive. Here we uncover a fundamental barrier to this transition and demonstrate a route to overcome it in computational quantum science, where advances shape both what we can learn from complex quantum systems and what quantum computing can ultimately deliver. Across 59 papers spanning classical simulation, quantum algorithms and quantum-property characterization, we systematically evaluate five agentic systems powered by large language models and six third-pa"

notes = "标题：SelfImprove-Bench: Evaluating Generalization in Self-Improving Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02797\n摘要：Recursively self-improving (RSI) systems are increasingly viewed as a promising means of accelerating progress toward artificial general intelligence. To this end, we propose SelfImprove-Bench,an RSI evaluation framework that organizes tasks into three primary capabilities (i.e., Exploring, Planning, Reasoning) as scenarios for guiding model evol"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究以首次正奖励时间和早期生成熵诊断 RLVR 的零到一跃迁，并提出 NAPO 利用高信息量负样本加速困难数学题的首次求解。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02747"
code = "https://github.com/jameszhu-pixel-time/Z2O"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究发现，困难提示在 RLVR 训练中可能长期无法获得正奖励，随后突然出现从未解到已解的离散“零到一”跃迁。事件分析表明，成功跃迁前的负向 rollout 通常具有更高的早期生成熵，可用于区分接近可解的失败轨迹与无效死路。作者据此提出 Negative-Augmented Policy Optimization（NAPO），在未解样本组中依据早期分支信号对负样本排序并赋予分级优势，从而弥补标准 GRPO 更新梯度消失的问题。在固定 rollout 预算下，NAPO 将 AIME24 和 AIME2025 的 Pass@256 分别提高 10.0 和 6.6 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 可将首次正奖励时间（TTFP）作为分析困难提示学习动态的事件级指标。\n- 早期生成熵能够识别具有潜在学习价值的负向轨迹，为稀疏奖励下的探索提供信号。\n- NAPO 展示了在全负样本组中构造分级优势、恢复有效训练梯度的策略。\n- 对数学推理模型训练而言，优先学习“接近成功”的失败轨迹可提高固定采样预算下的探索效率。"
notes = "标题：What Drives the Zero-to-One Jump? Diagnosing and Steering Hard-Prompt Learning in RLVR\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02747\n摘要：Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved mathematical reasoning, yet progress on hard prompts often stalls: a prompt can remain unsolved for many training steps before abruptly transitioning to solved--a discrete \"zero-to-one\" jump whose underlying dynamics and steerability remain poorly understood. We investigate two questions: what drives this transition, and can it be steered? Through event-based analysis measuring the time-to-first-positive (TTFP), we find that negative rollouts preceding successful transitions consistently retain higher early-stage generation entropy, distinguishing near-solvable failures from dead ends. This signal motivates NAPO (Negative-Augmented Policy Optimization), a method integrated with Group Relative Policy Optimizati"

content = "论文提出树式强化学习框架 TriGRPO，旨在缓解长程 LLM 智能体中成功经验稀缺和结果奖励稀疏的问题。框架利用后见引导分支：当轨迹到达匹配的决策状态时，以回滚中发现的成功动作创建可自主延续的兄弟分支。策略优化由根组、节点组和跨树组三类分组信号共同驱动，分别建模同一提示内的相对回报、父子与兄弟分支的结果差异，以及相关任务的历史表现。实验显示，该方法在不同模型规模上均能提升任务表现与回滚效率。"
purpose = "- 为稀疏奖励下的长程智能体训练提供更细粒度的信用分配机制。\n- 利用少量成功轨迹生成新的探索分支，提高高价值经验的复用效率。\n- 通过树内比较与跨任务历史反馈增强相对学习信号，可用于改进 Agentic RL 的样本效率。"
notes = "标题：Hindsight Credit Assignment over Trees for Agentic RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02706\n摘要：Reinforcement learning for long-horizon LLM agents faces a fundamental dilemma: successful experiences are essential for policy improvement, yet limited policy capabilities make such experiences difficult to discover. Sparse outcome rewards compound this challenge, leaving sampled trajectories with little relative feedback for learning. In this paper, we propose TriGRPO, a tree-based reinforcement learning framework that turns scarce successes into exploration and learning signals through hindsight credit assignment within and across rollout trees. TriGRPO constructs rollout trees through hindsight-guided branching: when an ongoing trajectory reaches a matched decision state, a successful action discovered during rollout initiates a sibling branch that continues autonomously, enabling furt"


[[items]]
id = "cue"
title = "CUE：通过自主机器人交互弥合动力学鸿沟"
subtopic = "主动学习"
topics = [

    "主动数据采集",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CUE 通过自主选择并执行信息量高的物理交互来辨识摩擦、恢复系数等隐藏参数，在无重置条件下持续校准数字孪生，并将台球位置误差相对最强基线降低 72%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02691"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CUE 面向 Agentic Real2Sim2Real 系统中仅靠视觉无法观测接触动力学参数的问题，将其定义为“动力学鸿沟”。系统只需接收高层指令，即可自主构建感知流程、重建数字场景，并辨识摩擦与恢复系数等物理参数。它会比较真实交互与模拟结果，优先选择模型分歧较大且安全可执行的交互，再利用观测持续更新仿真模型。在 25 种隐藏物理条件下，CUE 将留出击球的球位误差相对最强基线降低 72%，并在真实台球桌上完成部署验证。"
purpose = "- 展示利用主动数据采集弥补纯视觉观测不足、校准接触动力学模型的可行路径。\n- 为无重置、长时间运行的具身智能体提供“选择实验—执行交互—比较结果—更新模型”的闭环范式。\n- 可用于机器人数字孪生、仿真到现实迁移，以及摩擦、碰撞和恢复系数等物理参数的在线辨识。\n- 提示交互选择不仅需要不确定性评估，还需同时考虑安全性、可达性与实验信息量。"
notes = "标题：Closing the Dynamics Gap through Self-Directed Robotic Interaction\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02691\n摘要：Agentic Real2Sim2Real systems can reconstruct a scene in simulation from camera observations, but contact-rich dynamics such as friction and restitution cannot be observed without interaction. We call this the dynamics gap and present CUE, an agentic system that closes it by interacting with the environment itself. Given only a high-level instruction and no resets, the agent builds its own perception, identifies physical parameters, and targets interactions where its simulation is least accurate. On billiards, CUE reduces held-out ball-position error by 72% relative to the strongest baseline across 25 hidden-physics conditions in simulation, and we further deploy it on a real pool table. More details can be found on the project website: https://cue-dynamics.github.io/"

summary = "研究将四种策略内部奖励引入 GRPO，以增强模型最终答案对其生成推理过程的真实依赖，并揭示推理忠实性与任务性能之间的权衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02672"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者提出梯度敏感度、置信度差距、渐进式置信度增益和“推理—提示交换”四种轻量级策略内部奖励，并将其加入 GRPO。实验在两个小型指令微调模型上进行，使用 GSM8K 与 StrategyQA，通过推理编辑、释义和提示线索评估答案对推理过程的依赖程度。部分配置能够提高答案对推理编辑的敏感性，但准确率和释义鲁棒性的变化并不一致。高奖励权重可能诱发答案复制，而分解式推理场景也可能出现推理依赖度高但任务准确率低的现象，表明忠实性指标不能脱离任务表现单独解读。"
purpose = "- 为训练真正依赖生成式推理过程、而非仅生成表面合理 CoT 的模型提供轻量级奖励设计。\n- 可用于比较不同推理忠实性目标，并分析其与准确率、释义鲁棒性之间的权衡。\n- 实际应用中需联合监测答案复制、任务准确率和推理干预敏感性，避免通过奖励投机获得虚假的高依赖分数。"
notes = "标题：Chain of Faith: Policy-Internal Rewards for Reasoning Reliance\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02672\n摘要：Chain-of-thought (CoT) explanations can sound plausible while having little influence on a model's final answer. We study four rewards that use the policy's own signals to encourage reliance on generated reasoning: gradient sensitivity, confidence gaps, progressive confidence gains, and reasoning--prompt swaps. We add each reward to group relative policy optimization (GRPO) and evaluate two small instruction-tuned models on GSM8K and StrategyQA using reasoning edits, paraphrases, and prompt hints. Several configurations increase answer reliance to reasoning edits, while effects on accuracy and paraphrase robustness vary. We also observe answer copying at a high reward weight and high reliance in decomposition settings with poor task accuracy. The study compares practical objectives for rea"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02667"
code = ""

content = "- UPSHIFT比较同一会话中初始回答与反馈条件回答的隐藏表示，并将用户多次会话的轨迹位移平均为偏好方向。\n- 面对新查询时，该方法直接注入聚合后的偏好方向，无需更新模型参数、构建显式偏好档案或读取历史交互。\n- 其核心假设是：单次修订可能受到话题推进或任务纠错影响，但跨会话反复出现的调整包含稳定的用户偏好成分。\n- 在PrefEval的40个模拟用户上，Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B的全部测试配置均优于未干预基线，最佳配置将宏平均Elo分别从1364.0提升至1605.2、从1341.9提升至1591.8。"
purpose = "- 将日常交互中的自然反馈转化为轻量、可复用的个性化监督信号，降低偏好标注和参数微调成本。\n- 为不保留完整会话历史的隐私友好型个性化助手提供表征干预方案。\n- 匹配用户与正负方向对照实验表明，个性化效果来自用户特定偏好方向及其正确朝向，可为偏好表示验证提供实验范式。"
notes = "标题：UPSHIFT: User Preference Steering from Human-AI Interaction Feedback Traces\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02667\n摘要：Personalizing large language model assistants requires adapting their responses to individual users, including the selection of content, reasoning approach, level of detail, and form of presentation. Such preferences are often latent, becoming visible only when users respond to an initial answer and request revisions. We investigate whether these feedback trajectories can be converted into a reusable personalization signal without explicit preference profiles or labels, prompt-time access to interaction histories, or model parameter updates. We introduce UPSHIFT (User Preference Steering from Human-AI Interaction Feedback Traces), a training-free method that compares the hidden representations of initial and feedback-conditioned responses within each session, averages the resulting traject"

score = 3

summary = "BasisKV通过跨GPU联合优化低秩KV表示并复用压缩Value进行Key页面路由，同时降低长上下文推理的KV缓存占用与张量并行通信成本，最高实现1.42倍端到端加速。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02583"
code = "https://github.com/alexz949/BasisKV"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "BasisKV面向采用张量并行的长上下文推理，将低秩KV缓存压缩与多GPU通信优化统一考虑。该方法跨各GPU的注意力分区联合拟合低秩因子，以完整注意力输出为优化目标，而非独立压缩每个分区。压缩后的Value潜表示还被复用于预测Key路由信息，并以Key专用残差保存无法从Value恢复的部分，仅对选中的页面读取原始Key并计算精确QK分数。在多个模型家族和系统配置上，其质量—效率权衡优于既有低秩基线，并在8 GPU张量并行推理中取得最高1.42倍端到端加速。"
purpose = "- 为长上下文部署提供兼顾KV缓存容量、GPU间通信量和输出质量的联合优化方案。\n- 表明Value低秩表示不仅可用于缓存压缩，还可承担Key页面路由所需的部分信息预测。\n- 启示分布式推理优化不应孤立设计缓存压缩，而应围绕完整注意力输出联合考虑张量并行的数据布局与通信开销。\n- 可用于高并发、长上下文、多GPU推理服务的吞吐与成本优化。"
notes = "标题：BasisKV: Value Latents for Communication and Key Routing\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02583\n摘要：Long-context large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly constrained by the cost of maintaining an ever-growing KV-cache. Low-rank K/V projections provide a promising way to compress the KV-cache with minimal quality loss, but prior work has largely optimized KV-cache compression in isolation, leaving distributed inference, especially with tensor parallelism where attention computation is partitioned across multiple GPUs, largely underexplored. We make two observations: (i) tensor-parallel communication is an important cost when deploying low-rank KV-cache compression in distributed inference; and (ii) Value compression can be associated with the output projection, allowing the same low-rank Value representation to reduce both Value-cache width and tensor-parallel communication. B"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02574"
code = ""

content = "研究在GSM8K上追踪OLMo-2-1B的13个RLVR训练检查点，首次作答准确率由58.0%升至61.3%。在每题采样256次时，解法覆盖率从94.5%升至96.9%，说明正确解题知识在训练前已基本存在。针对3类人工植入的推理错误，简单探针在各检查点的AUROC稳定在0.798至0.840，未显示模型形成了更清晰的错误表征。显著内部变化主要出现在最终答案词元：区分早期与晚期检查点的探针AUROC达到0.93，而对实际推理步骤的区分接近随机水平。"
purpose = "- 支持RLVR的“锐化”解释：训练主要重排既有推理路径的输出概率，而非创造新的推理能力。\n- 评估RLVR时应同时报告首答准确率、重复采样覆盖率与内部表征变化，避免将采样策略改善误判为能力增长。\n- 为分析推理训练机制提供逐检查点追踪、错误植入和表征探测相结合的实验范式。\n- 提示后续训练方法应关注推理过程中的内部表征学习，而不只是最终答案奖励。"
notes = "标题：RLVR Sharpens the Pencil, Not the Mind\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02574\n摘要：Models like DeepSeek-R1 made Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) famous: reward a model for correct answers and it gets dramatically better at reasoning without being taught how. The leading explanation is sharpening---that the base model already knows how to solve these problems and RLVR just makes it more likely to reach for its best solution paths. This has only ever been checked by comparing before and after training, never by watching what changes inside across it. We tracked OLMo-2-1B across all 13 training checkpoints on GSM8K. First-try accuracy climbs from 58.0% to 61.3%, but ask the model 256 times and solution coverage actually rises from 94.5% to 96.9%, proving the knowledge was already there. We then planted reasoning mistakes into problems and trained simple"

code = ""

content = "论文提出工具级自演化防御框架 EVOLVE，将智能体安全建模为累积式安全演化过程。该框架把交互中获得的安全经验转化为可执行护栏工具，并通过持续继承、独立审计和迭代优化实现终身更新。跨多个基准、骨干模型和具身环境的实验显示，EVOLVE 在多样化风险场景中的平均 F1 分数比现有防御方案高 22%。作者还在真实机器人与自主智能体系统中验证了该框架，表明持续演化的防御机制可能比静态规则、提示或分类器更适合开放世界安全。"
purpose = "- 为开放世界智能体构建能够随新风险持续更新的安全防线。\n- 将安全经验沉淀为可迁移、可执行的护栏工具，减少对静态规则和固定分类器的依赖。\n- 通过独立审计约束护栏演化过程，兼顾防御能力积累与更新可靠性。\n- 为机器人及自主智能体的长期部署提供持续安全改进思路。"
notes = "标题：Cumulative and Audited Guardrail Tool Evolution for LLM Agent Safety\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02516\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in open-world environments where safety risks cannot be fully predefined. However, most existing guardrail mechanisms remain static, relying on fixed rules, prompts, or classifiers that generalize poorly to emerging risks. Here we present EVOLVE, a tool-level self-evolving defense framework that formulates agent security as a process of cumulative security evolution. Inspired by how human societies transform experiences into transferable protective knowledge, EVOLVE externalizes security knowledge into executable guardrail tools that are continuously inherited, independently audited, and iteratively refined through lifelong interaction. Across multiple benchmarks, backbone models, and embodied environments, EVOLVE consistently outperfo"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，禁止特定输出 token 无法阻止潜意识特征传播，隐藏特征会转移至其他可用通道，但干预负责写入特征的注意力头与神经元回路可显著削弱传播。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02509"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究通过偏好链与名称链两类压力测试，在两个模型、六种输出格式和 16 种特征上逐一封禁隐藏特征的外显通道。结果显示，表层 token 禁令不能消除特征，特征会转移到格式允许的其他位置，形成类似挤压气球的“气球效应”。作者将传播机制分解至单个注意力头和神经元，定位到负责把隐藏特征写入输出的内部回路。对该回路进行激活修补后，传播链被阻断，显式名称泄漏显著减少，学生模型继承的特征约降低一半，而随机干预相同数量的注意力头没有明显效果。"
purpose = "- 说明仅依赖敏感 token 过滤或输出格式约束，无法可靠阻止教师模型向学生模型传递隐藏偏好。\n- 为潜意识学习和蒸馏偏置提供回路级机制解释，可用于识别训练数据中不可见的行为倾向传播。\n- 提示安全对齐应从表层内容审查扩展到内部回路定位、激活修补和传播链验证。\n- 可据此设计隐藏特征传播压力测试，评估模型蒸馏及合成数据训练的安全性。"
notes = "标题：A balloon effect: ban one channel and a hidden trait finds another\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02509\n摘要：Language models trained on the outputs of other models can acquire traits that those outputs never mention, such as a preference for an animal carried by lists of numbers. Such subliminal learning can pass on unwanted traits. The intuitive defence is to find and forbid the tokens through which a trait shows, but how a trait's trace behaves under such bans, and what of it a student learns, has not been resolved. We closed a trait's outlets one by one in two stress tests, a preference chain and a name chain, across two models, six output formats and 16 traits. Bans did not remove the trait. Much as squeezing a balloon in one place makes it bulge in another, the trait moved to wherever the format still allowed, an effect we call the balloon effect. Decomposing this transfer, which bans on sur"

summary = "SpecPilot通过成本模型、并行智能体、逐词元验证和结构化记忆，自主发现适配具体部署环境的无损投机解码优化，将单请求与批处理加速比分别提升至10.87倍和3.93倍。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02501"
code = ""

content = "SpecPilot构建了一个自主智能体闭环，针对不同服务负载、草稿器、目标模型和GPU自动探索算法及系统优化。系统利用成本模型定位当前实现与理论性能上限之间的最大差距，再由并行智能体探索方案并整合已验证的改进。逐词元强制验证会将所有输出与原始系统对照，以检测奖励作弊；结构化记忆则负责跨轮次、跨部署复用有效机制。该框架发现了无损12位权重格式、面向混合循环目标模型的树验证、两级验证及三种批处理机制，在无须训练且不损失质量的情况下，将单请求加速比从4.46倍提高至10.87倍，批处理加速比从1.94倍提高至3.93倍。"
purpose = "- 展示智能体闭环可用于自动发现部署相关的推理算法与底层系统优化，减少人工调优成本。\n- 通过成本模型优先定位性能瓶颈，为受计算资源约束的优化搜索提供明确方向。\n- 逐词元对照验证可防止以输出质量或正确性换取虚假加速，为自主系统优化提供可靠验收机制。\n- 结构化记忆能够沉淀已验证的优化机制，并将其迁移到新的模型、硬件和服务配置中。"
notes = "标题：SpecPilot: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery and Systems Optimization for Speculative Decoding\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02501\n摘要：Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM decoding with a lightweight drafter, and parallel drafters extend this speedup by drafting a whole block in one forward pass. However, it remains unclear whether their released systems (stock) attain the full speedup that parallel drafting permits. We identify three challenges. First, with one request, each step accepts few tokens but reads every target weight. Second, with batching, per-request verification work roughly halves the speedup over autoregressive (AR) decoding. Third, speculative decoding is deployment-specific, and no universal optimization fits every serving regime, drafter, target model and GPU. To address these challenges, we present SpecPilot, an autonomous agentic loop that discovers algorithms and systems optimizations f"

content = "RecRSI是一个基于LLM的推荐算法研究框架，以机制假设而非孤立的参数或代码修改来组织算法发现。每个假设将机制变更与参考设计、设计理由及预期实验现象关联起来，MechSpace则从任务源中提取可实现的基本组件与组合规则。Research Line结合假设空间和历史证据设计并检验后续方案，Mechanism Feedback将实验结果及其设计上下文传递到下一轮研究。在4种初始推荐器和4个数据集上，该框架相对初始算法将NDCG@5和HR@5平均提升8.48%和6.88%，相对各设置下最强竞争结果分别提升2.60%和2.20%。"

notes = "标题：RecRSI: Mechanism-Guided Discovery of Recommendation Algorithms\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02487\n摘要：Improving recommendation algorithms for the Web requires coordinating interacting design choices and learning from experiments. Each hypothesis links a proposed mechanism change to a reference design, a rationale, and an expected observation. MechSpace derives implementable primitives and composition rules from the task source; Research Line uses this space and prior evidence to develop and test hypotheses; Mechanism Feedback carries outcomes and their design context into subsequent research. This organization makes each experiment inform which mechanisms to develop, revise, or combine next. Across four initial recommenders and four datasets, RecRSI yields average relative imp"

summary = "RLiD直接优化可执行动作选项的概率，消除自然语言动作表达带来的策略梯度方差，并通过轨迹蒸馏兼顾智能体性能与生成式部署。"

content = "RLiD将观察、可选的紧凑记忆和带标签的动作菜单编译为输入，由大决策模型在单次前向传播中直接选择可执行动作。相比词元级强化学习，它绕过自回归生成与动作解析，避免无效动作输出，并降低同一动作不同语言表述造成的梯度方差。理论分析表明，对同一动作的多种文本实现进行边缘化，可以去除零均值梯度分量而不改变期望更新。实验覆盖ARC、Countdown、Sokoban和ALFWorld；完整训练与蒸馏流程在ALFWorld上以12.7个H100 GPU小时取得99.3%成功率，蒸馏后的Qwen2.5-1.5B模型配合编译记忆在全部已见和未见评测游戏上达到100%成功率。"
purpose = "- 将强化学习的优化对象从自然语言词元序列对齐到环境实际执行的离散动作，有助于改善信用分配并降低策略梯度方差。\n- 单次前向决策可减少自回归解码、动作解析和无效轨迹带来的训练成本，适用于动作空间明确的语言智能体。\n- 通过动作轨迹监督将决策模型蒸馏回生成式LLM，为高效训练与通用部署之间提供了一条可行路径。\n- 编译式紧凑记忆与动作菜单结合，可用于提升长程交互环境中的状态利用效率和执行可靠性。"
notes = "标题：RLiD: Reinforcement Learning in the Decision Plane for Language Agents and LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02455\n摘要：Reinforcement learning (RL) has improved the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) across a wide range of tasks. However, token-level RL assigns an action's advantage to the entire generated response, including tokens specifying the parsed action, reasoning chains, and malformed content. Although the environment executes only the parsed action, variation in how the action is expressed in natural language can introduce additional variance into policy-gradient estimates. We propose RLiD (Reinforcement Learning in the Decision plane), a framework that directly optimizes the probabilities of executable action options. RLiD compiles observations, an optional compact memory, and a labeled action menu into the model input, and uses a large decision model (LDM) to select an action in a sing"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02440"
code = ""

content = "SessionFarmer 联合分析对话与提交历史，过滤无关会话，并将跨轮次、跨会话的交互聚合为围绕单一工程目标的工作片段。系统为每个片段重建隔离执行容器，综合完整上下文生成任务描述、仓库级质量量规和单元测试。其闭环测试改进机制利用破坏需求的变异补丁和盲求解器，减少对有效替代方案的误拒绝以及对不完整方案的误接受。基于该框架构建的 CropChat 包含来自 61 个公共仓库、覆盖 14 种编程语言的 242 项任务；12 种模型与脚手架配置中最佳 Pass@1 为 39.3%，同一模型仅因脚手架差异即可产生最高 8.4 个百分点的波动。"
purpose = "- 将企业或开源项目中的真实编程协作记录转化为贴近内部工作流的可复现评测任务。\n- 通过完整工作片段而非单轮对话恢复需求，降低任务描述遗漏和评测失真。\n- 揭示编程智能体成绩对执行脚手架高度敏感，评测时应同时审计模型、工具链与运行环境配置。\n- 可用于持续构建仓库级私有基准，并借助闭环测试改进提高验收测试的区分能力。"
notes = "标题：Harvesting SWE Benchmarks from Wild Vibe Coding Sessions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02440\n摘要：Development teams need reproducible tasks from their own repositories and workflows to evaluate coding agents. Conversational developer-agent collaboration (\"vibe coding\") produces abundant coding sessions and repository checkpoints, but requirements evolve across turns, unrelated conversations co-occur with commits, and engineering efforts can span multiple sessions. We present SessionFarmer, an automated framework that turns these records into self-contained, executable software engineering tasks. It analyzes dialogue and commit history to filter unrelated sessions and group multi-turn, potentially multi-session interactions into work episodes centered on one engineering goal. For each episode, it reconstructs a hermetic execution container, synthesizes a task statement from the full con"

summary = "EvoMemState 通过关系状态与请求相关的推理状态，将个人智能体长期记忆从独立事实集合升级为可动态演化和按需应用的状态系统。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03743"
code = "https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/EvoMemState.git"
dataset = ""

content = "EvoMemState 针对事实中心记忆难以表达事实间更新、约束和冲突的问题，提出状态中心的个人智能体长期记忆框架。其本体引导的记忆状态归纳模块抽取持久事实，并构建记录有效性、适用范围和演化关系的关系状态。框架利用强化学习将记忆使用经验内化为策略，为当前请求生成专属的推理状态，再结合相关事实与关系状态指导回答。长期对话和记忆使用基准实验显示，该方法能够提升记忆检索完整性及基于记忆的回答准确率。"
purpose = "- 为个人智能体提供比独立事实存储更具结构性和一致性的长期记忆表示。\n- 利用关系状态显式追踪事实的有效性、作用范围、更新关系与潜在冲突。\n- 通过强化学习生成请求相关的推理状态，使智能体能够判断记忆的适用方式和优先级。\n- 可用于持续个性化助手、长期对话系统及需要可靠用户状态维护的智能体。"
notes = "标题：EvoMemState: Evolving Memory State for Personal Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03743\n摘要：Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to provide continuous personalized assistance by retaining user information across interactions. Existing systems support this process through fact extraction, organization, updating, and retrieval, but largely maintain memories as independent facts. This fact-centric paradigm may overlook how new facts update, constrain, or conflict with earlier ones, while leaving their applicability, role, and priority for the current request implicit. In this paper, we introduce EvoMemState, a state-centric framework that augments persistent facts with two complementary states: Relational States that capture how facts remain valid and relate to one another as memory evolves, and a Reasoning State that determines how these facts should be applie"
